市场监管总局9月6日公布第二批共7起破除妨碍统一市场和公平竞争案件，涉及医药卫生、能源、殡葬、垃圾清运、互联网上网服务等领域，相关行政机关已停止违法行为并整改到位。

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 7, 00:26

**「专项行动背景」** 专项行动于今年5月起开展，聚焦纠治歧视性准入条件、区域壁垒、排他性合作协议、拖延审批等四类行政垄断行为，违反《反垄断法》关于行政机关不得滥用权力排除、限制竞争的规定。

**「对市场主体影响」** 上述行为增加企业制度性交易成本，限制外地经营者参与本地市场竞争；下一步市场监管总局将加大查办力度，为各类经营主体创造更公平的竞争环境。

Eight design institutes and twenty-two design groups now collaborate in a unified 3D environment with automated clash detection, allowing teams to pre-simulate interactions such as pile foundations passing near a high-speed rail tunnel. **「Impact」** For teams working on large, multi-disciplinary transport hubs, the reported workflow can reduce design conflicts and rework before construction begins. **「Impact」** The infusion significantly strengthens the risk buffers and lending capacity of China&\#x27;s largest state-owned banks and major insurers. ### [August logistics activity expands slightly](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/07/content_30179385.html) ⭐️ 1.0/10

China’s logistics sector expanded in August 2026, with the China Federation of Logistics and Purchasing reporting the Logistics Industry Business Activity Index at 50.9 percent, up 0.5 percentage points from July and remaining above the 50.0 percent expansion threshold. Business volume, new orders, and employment indices all stayed in expansion territory, while regional spreads remained narrow across eastern, central, and western areas. Demand growth was concentrated in electrical machinery, pharmaceuticals, automotive parts, and electronic components manufacturing, whereas bulk energy logistics stabilized after several months of flat readings.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 7, 00:26

**「Background」** The Logistics Industry Business Activity Index is a monthly survey-based indicator published by the China Federation of Logistics and Purchasing that tracks freight activity, order inflows, capacity utilization, and employment in China’s logistics sector. A reading above 50.0 percent typically signals month-over-month expansion, while readings below that level indicate contraction, making it a widely followed gauge of domestic trade and industrial supply-chain momentum.

**「Impact」** The marginal index improvement suggests near-term stability for logistics operators and supply-chain service providers handling manufactured goods, though it does not imply broad-based acceleration. The sustained regional balance may support ongoing coordination among China’s major logistics corridors but offers limited guidance for outside markets.

A Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung column explains that under German co-determination rules, whether a capital company must establish a supervisory board with employee representatives depends on its headcount, and only employees of subsidiaries count toward that threshold under strict conditions. **「Background」** German co-determination law ties the obligation to create a mixed supervisory board to employee thresholds, so the definition of which workers are included can decide whether companies must share board-level control. In German corporate groups, only certain parent-company staff are normally counted, while subsidiary employees are included only when specific legal conditions apply.

**「Impact」** German corporate groups with many subsidiary employees may avoid triggering mandatory worker representation on their supervisory board if those staff do not meet the narrow inclusion conditions. Companies and labor representatives should therefore verify exactly which entities and employment relationships are counted when assessing co-determination obligations.

As of August 2026, the Sanxingdui Museum文物修复保护团队 has cleaned and restored over 1,000 ivory, bronze, jade, and other artifacts from the newly excavated sacrificial pits since 2021. The team, averaging about 36 years old, has grown from four or five members in 2010 to more than ten, covering the full chain from analysis and data collection to repair planning. They meticulously monitor buried ivory stored in a cold, humid underground vault and combine hands-on conservation with scientific techniques such as X-ray imaging. Cross-disciplinary newcomers, including a 2000-born chemist, are strengthening the museum&\#x27;s analytical capabilities while younger conservators continue a long apprenticeship tradition.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 7, 00:26

**「Background」** Sanxingdui is an ancient Shu civilization site in Sichuan province known for unusually large bronze, jade, ivory, and gold objects. Modern Chinese museum conservation now blends traditional manual restoration with laboratory analysis, condition monitoring, and documentation to stabilize fragile organic and inorganic materials.

**「Impact」** The museum&\#x27;s growing conservation capacity improves the long-term stability and research availability of the newly discovered Sanxingdui assemblage and allows the site to provide mobile cultural-relic conservation support to other institutions. Its results are described as filling gaps in domestic cultural heritage conservation practice.

### [China approves graded protection guide for museum animal specimens](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/07/content_30179449.html) ⭐️ 0.0/10

The Chinese Society of Natural History Museums approved the project立项 for the \*Guide to Graded Protection and Display of Animal Specimens in Museums\* as part of its 2026 second batch of group standards. The guide classifies specimens into three protection levels, requiring Level 1 protected animal specimens to be displayed in enclosed cabinets and prohibiting bare display, while allowing conditional bare display for lower-risk specimens with appropriate barriers and signage. It mandates that protective requirements be integrated into exhibition design from the outset to control risks such as touching,拍打, and tearing. The guide also establishes protocols for registering, isolating, and assessing damaged specimens for professional repair.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 7, 00:26

**「Background」** Museum animal specimens are fragile cultural and scientific heritage objects that require careful handling to balance public accessibility with long-term preservation. China&\#x27;s natural history museums have increasingly faced challenges from visitor interaction damaging irreplaceable specimens, prompting the need for standardized protection guidelines.

**「Impact」** Museums displaying animal specimens in China will need to revise exhibition designs to comply with the graded protection requirements, particularly ensuring Level 1 protected specimens are placed in enclosed cabinets. Exhibition designers and museum curators must integrate protective measures during the planning phase rather than as an afterthought.

**Tags**: `#museum curation`, `#standards`, `#cultural heritage`, `#specimen preservation`, `#non-technology`

<a id="item-tech-news-186"></a>
### [Revolutionary-era rail link opens between Ruijin and Yan&\#x27;an](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/07/content_30179384.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 6, 2026, the first direct Fuxing high-speed train, G3998, departed Ruijin in Jiangxi for Yan&\#x27;an in Shaanxi, completing the 1,800-plus-kilometer journey in 11 hours 32 minutes. The service connects the Central Red Army&\#x27;s Long March departure point with its final destination, routing through 13 stops across seven stitched railway lines including the Gan-Ruilong, Beijing-Kunming, and Xi&\#x27;an-Yan&\#x27;an high-speed corridors. By enabling same-day travel between the two historically significant cities, the route provides a dedicated transportation backbone for red-themed educational trips, tourism cooperation, and coordinated development of the revolutionary base areas.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 7, 00:26

**「Background」** The Central Red Army began its Long March from Ruijin in 1934 and reached Yan&\#x27;an in northern Shaanxi after more than a year of movement. Today, those routes are linked by China&\#x27;s expanding high-speed rail network, which often combines multiple regional lines to serve cities that previously required longer, indirect journeys.

**「Impact」** The new直达 high-speed service gives passengers traveling between Ruijin and Yan&\#x27;an a reliable same-day rail option instead of multi-day or transfer-heavy itineraries. It also strengthens practical links for educators, tour operators, and local governments coordinating red tourism and regional development along the former Long March corridor.

<a id="item-tech-news-198"></a>
### [China advocates inclusive AI governance at 2026 World AI Conference](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/07/content_30179438.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 7, 2026, People&\#x27;s Daily published a special commentary by Zhang Xiao, deputy director of the China Internet Network Information Center, outlining China&\#x27;s approach to global artificial intelligence governance. The article highlights President Xi Jinping&\#x27;s address at the 2026 World AI Conference, which called for AI to become a driving force for common prosperity and security while building a fair global governance system. China has established the World AI Cooperation Organization as the first intergovernmental AI organization, launched capacity-building programs for developing nations, and pledged 5,000 training slots over five years to bridge the digital divide. The piece emphasizes multilateral cooperation, open technology sharing, and inclusive development as alternatives to technological exclusion and hegemony.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 7, 00:26

**「Background」** Global AI governance has become a critical challenge as artificial intelligence rapidly evolves faster than regulatory frameworks can adapt. Developing nations face increasing capability gaps in AI adoption and governance compared to advanced economies, prompting calls for more equitable international cooperation mechanisms.

**「Impact」** China&\#x27;s establishment of the World AI Cooperation Organization and capacity-building initiatives provides developing countries with institutional pathways to participate equally in AI governance and benefit from technological advancement.

### [AfD Falls Short of Majority in Saxony-Anhalt](https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/06/world/europe/germany-afd-election-saxony-anhalt.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Germany&\#x27;s Alternative for Germany \(AfD\) won the September 2026 state election in Saxony-Anhalt by a significant margin over rival parties. Despite this strong performance, the party fell short of securing an outright majority or the ability to easily form a stable governing coalition. The election outcome has been described as shocking for Germany, highlighting the continued rise of the far right while also demonstrating electoral limits to its expansion.

rss · 纽约时报\(The New York Times\) · Sep 6, 23:00

**「Political Context」** Saxony-Anhalt is one of Germany&\#x27;s eastern states where the AfD has historically performed stronger than in western regions. State elections in Germany determine regional governments and can serve as barometers for national political trends ahead of federal elections.

**「Political Consequences」** The AfD&\#x27;s near-majority result complicates governability in Saxony-Anhalt and intensifies debates about coalition options for the far-right party. Other German political forces are likely to reinforce their cordon sanitaire against partnering with the AfD at the state level.

### [Trump tariffs compound climate threats to Canada&\#x27;s honey industry](https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/06/world/canada/trump-tariffs-canada-bees-honey.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Alberta, which produces 40 percent of Canada&\#x27;s honey, faces a dual threat to its apiary industry this year from both adverse weather and escalating trade tensions. Rainfall has already impacted beekeeping conditions, but the situation has deteriorated further due to President Donald Trump&\#x27;s tariff policies directed at Canadian goods. The combination of these environmental and economic pressures is increasingly endangering the livelihoods of producers in Canada&\#x27;s primary honey-producing region.

rss · 纽约时报\(The New York Times\) · Sep 6, 11:01

**「Background」** Alberta is Canada&\#x27;s largest honey-producing region, responsible for approximately 40 percent of the nation&\#x27;s honey output. The U.S. is a major export destination for Alberta beekeepers, with some farms sending between a third and half of their production south of the border.

### [Europeans Seek Americana on Route 66&\#x27;s Centennial](https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/06/business/route-66-highway-centennial.html) ⭐️ ?/10

As Route 66 marks its 100th anniversary, a growing number of European tourists are visiting the historic U.S. highway to experience authentic Americana. These travelers are drawn to the iconic road as a way to temporarily escape the influences of global conflicts, rising inflation, and political tensions in their home countries. The centennial celebration highlights the enduring international appeal of this legendary American route.

rss · 纽约时报\(The New York Times\) · Sep 6, 10:47

**「Background」** U.S. Route 66, originally established in 1926 and largely decommissioned in 1985, is a historic highway that once connected Chicago to Santa Monica. Today, large segments of the road still exist as local streets or preserved tourist routes, allowing international visitors to experience the &\#x27;Americana&\#x27; cultural nostalgia the route represents.

### [Aeroflot to receive first MS-21 aircraft in late 2027](https://max.ru/tass_agency) ⭐️ ?/10

Aeroflot CEO Sergey Sanyagin announced at the Eastern Economic Forum that the airline plans to receive its first eight MS-21 aircraft in the fourth quarter of 2027, with an additional 10 planes expected in 2028. A second batch of 90 MS-21 aircraft under an existing lease, supply, and maintenance contract will begin deliveries in 2029, with all 90 to be received by the end of 2032. The domestic jets will initially operate on short-haul routes such as Moscow–Saint Petersburg and Moscow–Nizhny Novgorod, while pilot, cabin crew, and engineer training for the MS-21 is scheduled to start in 2027.

telegram · tass\_agency · Sep 6, 21:10

**「Background」** The MS-21 is a Russian wide-body jet developed by Irkut \(part of the United Aircraft Corporation\) as part of Russia&\#x27;s effort to modernize its domestic aviation industry and reduce reliance on Western aircraft. The program has faced delays due to international sanctions affecting engine and composite materials supply, leading to the development of Russian alternatives.

**「Impact」** Aeroflot&\#x27;s MS-21 integration will begin phased operations in late 2027 on select short-haul domestic routes, marking a step toward fleet diversification away from Western manufacturers. The full delivery of 90 additional MS-21s by 2032 could significantly expand Aeroflot&\#x27;s domestic capacity using locally produced aircraft.

<a id="item-tech-blog-2"></a>
### [Competence Requires Maintenance Under AI Offloading](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/Sj5x4we53LigxFsxp/competence-has-a-maintenance-requirement) ⭐️ 5.0/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 6, 23:22

**「Background」** Organizations increasingly rely on AI automation to reduce cognitive load, creating an assumption that humans can seamlessly reclaim control when needed. This assumes competence is static, ignoring that offloaded skills silently erode without deliberate upkeep.

**「Solution」** The author builds on Lisanne Bainbridge’s 1983 ‘Ironies of Automation,’ which identified how automation can degrade human operator situational awareness and manual skills. The essay argues that cognitive offloading to AI generates similar silent organizational exposure: as AI handles more tasks, human operators lose the practiced competence required to intervene during failures. However, the piece offers no concrete mechanisms, empirical evidence, or implementation guidance beyond restating this premise.

**「Takeaway」** Competence gained through direct human involvement has a maintenance requirement; without deliberate practice, offloading to AI creates hidden fragility in systems where human oversight remains essential.

**Tags**: `#AI offloading`, `#automation skill atrophy`, `#systemic risk`, `#human-AI collaboration`

### [Symbolic Value of an Old Racket in Family Bonding](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/07/content_30179454.html) ⭐️ 1.0/10

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 7, 00:26

**「Background」** The September 2026 &quot;Village Table Tennis&quot; event in Ningxia captured a tender mother-son interaction that revealed how sports can strengthen family ties beyond competition.

**「Solution」** The editorial describes how years earlier, the mother accompanied her first-grade son Bolin to table tennis lessons and supported his training journey. Now, the skilled son has reversed roles, teaching his mother grip and correcting her posture. The old racket serves as a tangible symbol of this evolving relationship—once she was behind him collecting balls, now he is the knowledgeable presence courtside. Their conversations about the sport continue over phone calls, and holiday practice sessions involve repeated adjustments. The mother&\#x27;s simple advice—&\#x27;persisting makes it worthwhile&\#x27;—carries emotional weight beyond its plain phrasing.

**「Takeaway」** For ordinary people, sports matter less about skill level and more about enriching daily life and keeping relationships vibrant through shared passion and mutual growth.
